On held-out Retail tasks, BTM improves robustness by up to 16.8 percentage points without retraining, while RL learns complementary recovery behavior that remains beneficial without inference-time BTM. Combining the two reaches 40.8-45.5% under injection while preserving failure-free performance. These results suggest that robust tool use benefits from combining environment-specific recovery knowledge with learned recovery behavior.

</details>


### [100] [Claim-Level Reliability Assessment for Efficient Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11994)
*Sen Xu,Wei Wang,Shixi Liu,Jixin Min,Yingwei Dai,Zhibin Yin,Yirong Chen,Junlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“声明级证伪”测试时扩展原则，并以无需训练的声明级可靠性评估框架CLR实现。CLR将推理轨迹压缩为关键决策声明，针对这些声明寻找反证，以更高效地识别和抑制高置信度错误推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试时扩展通常依赖采样更多完整解答并进行整体轨迹评估，但常规文本会稀释决定性错误信号，导致错误推理可能形成表面共识。在固定模型能力下，生成完全正确的推理链较困难，而证伪错误声明只需找到一个决定性缺陷，因此需要将计算资源从重复生成转向定向验证。

Method: CLR首先从每条推理轨迹中提取紧凑的、决定结论的关键声明，以隔离逻辑锚点；随后对这些声明进行语义层面的证伪搜索，收集负面证据。框架利用“构造正确解需全程无误、推翻错误声明只需单点漏洞”的非对称性，通过非线性可靠性评分压缩错误高置信轨迹的存活空间，并据此重新评估和选择候选答案。该方法不需要额外训练。

Result: 在4个大语言模型和4个推理基准、且测试预算匹配的条件下，CLR总体优于pass@1和自洽性采样。以GPT-OSS-20B在CMIMC25上的结果为例，CLR相对pass@1提高27.15个百分点；相对自洽性方法，准确率从77.50%提升至82.19%，同时令牌消耗减少37.0%。

Conclusion: CLR表明，测试时计算不必主要用于增加完整解答样本数，而可通过对关键声明实施定向证伪获得更高的可靠性与效率。其核心贡献是将推理验证粒度从整条轨迹下沉到声明层面，并利用反证搜索降低错误共识的影响。

Abstract: We propose claim-level falsification as a principle for test-time scaling and instantiate it through Claim-Level Reliability Assessment (CLR), a training-free framework that reallocates test-time compute from additional solution sampling to targeted verification. Since whole-trace evaluation often obscures decisive errors due to signal dilution from routine tokens, CLR condenses each reasoning trace into a compact set of decision-critical claims, thereby isolating its logical anchors. Furthermore, recognizing the inherent difficulty of generating entirely correct solutions under fixed model capabilities, CLR shifts the focus to semantic falsification. This approach exploits a fundamental asymmetry between solution construction and claim refutation. Constructing a valid solution requires a flawless reasoning path, whereas refuting an incorrect claim requires identifying only a single decisive flaw. This targeted search for negative evidence systematically compresses the survival space of high-confidence incorrect traces, effectively suppressing erroneous consensus via nonlinear reliability scoring. Across four LLMs and four reasoning benchmarks under matched budgets, CLR generally improves upon pass@1 and self-consistency. On GPT-OSS-20B/CMIMC25, for instance, CLR exceeds pass@1 by 27.15 percentage-points and raises self-consistency accuracy from 77.50\% to 82.19\% with 37.0\% fewer tokens.

</details>


### [101] [CTBench: Evaluating Troubleshooting Capabilities of AI Agents in Realistic Telecom Network Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12002)
*Xingyu Yan,Tingting Dai,Antonio De Domenico,Mohamed Sana,Nicola Piovesan,Changchang Li,Bowen Liu,Kun Jiang,Mengjie Zhang,Dingcheng Shan,Jing-Cheng Pang,Chenwei Wu,Sijie Wu,Lianying Chao,Haoran Cai,Jiantao Ye,Xubin Li,Simon Mark Lucas,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CTBench，一个用于评估智能体在电信网络运维与故障排障能力的公开基准，重点覆盖根因分析与路径恢复。结果表明，现有先进智能体在路径恢复的端点识别上表现较好，但在根因分析及基于证据的诊断方面明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测通常无法真实反映电信网络的复杂性，包括多厂商设备、异构协议、不同管理接口和部分可观测环境。同时，网络运维不仅要求给出最终处置答案，还要求提供可验证的诊断证据，以满足实际运营中的可靠性、审计和风险控制要求。因此，需要面向真实电信排障流程的专门基准。

Method: 作者构建CTBench，将任务划分为根因分析和路径恢复两类，并由领域专家设计任务、标注丰富元数据及黄金证据步骤。评测采用专家定义的指标，同时衡量智能体最终答案的正确性与诊断过程是否覆盖关键证据。实验比较了多种代表性智能体框架与模型组合，并进一步分析其资源消耗与诊断效果之间的关系。

Result: 实验发现，先进智能体在路径恢复任务中较擅长识别需要操作的端点，但整体而言在根因分析任务上的表现较弱。其尤其难以处理接口状态、链路层、业务管理及其他运行维护类故障。即使智能体能够生成看似合理甚至正确的最终结论，也经常缺少支撑该结论的关键证据链。路径恢复通常需要更多计算或交互资源，但资源投入增加并不必然提升诊断质量。

Conclusion: CTBench揭示了当前智能体与合格电信排障工程师之间的重要差距：问题不仅在于最终答案准确率，更在于能否进行可追溯、证据驱动且符合运维实践的诊断。未来研究应加强智能体在部分可观测网络环境中的状态推理、跨层故障定位、工具使用效率和证据链生成能力，而不能仅依赖增加推理或交互资源。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly considered for automating network operations and maintenance, where engineers must diagnose network faults, optimize configurations to enhance services, and reduce operational costs while acting under strict constraints. However, existing evaluations fail to accurately model real network characteristics or assess agents under partially observable telecom environments with diverse vendors, devices, protocols, and interfaces. In this paper, we introduce CTBench, a public benchmark for assessing whether an agent behaves like a competent telecom troubleshooting engineer. CTBench focuses on root cause analysis and path restoration. Each task is constructed by experts and annotated with rich task metadata, including golden evidence steps. CTBench uses expert-grounded metrics that evaluate both final answers and the diagnostic evidence. Experiments with representative harness-model combinations show that state-of-the-art agents perform very well at identifying endpoints in path-restoration tasks but, more generally, underperform in root cause analysis. In particular, agents struggle with interface state, link-layer, service-management, and other operational faults. Most importantly, even when agents produce plausible or correct final answers, they often fail to provide the evidence-grounded diagnoses required in operational practice. Our results further show that path restoration is generally more resource expensive, yet larger resource usage does not necessarily translate into better diagnosis.

</details>


### [102] [Mechanist: AI as a Scientific Instrument for Discovering the Mechanisms of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12036)
*Mengru Wang,Junfeng Fang,Shuofei Qiao,Zhenqian Xu,Haoming Xu,Haoxiong Wang,Shumin Deng,Linyi Yang,Zhixiang Cui,Xin Xu,Yunzhi Yao,Buqiang Xu,Fei Shen,Haozhe Luo,Yunxiang Wei,Ningyu Zhang,Julian McAuley,Tat Seng Chua,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Mechanist，一种面向AI机制自主发现的智能体系统。它结合解释性知识图谱、多学科文献库和机制分析方法库，能够自动提出机制假设、设计并执行实验，逐步实现从发现模型行为到解释和控制模型的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型能力快速提升，但其内部机制、风险来源及可控性仍缺乏系统理解；传统机制解释主要依赖人工，难以匹配模型研发速度。因此，论文旨在借助AI自动化机制科学研究，缩小模型能力与理解、控制能力之间的差距。

Method: 构建包含约1.3万篇论文的可解释性知识图谱，并接入覆盖26个领域、约4300万篇论文的综合文献数据库；整理32种用于机制分析、因果干预和结果验证的基础方法。Mechanist以智能体形式完成文献检索、机制假设生成、实验规划、代码执行和结果验证，并与Claude Code及已有AI科学家系统进行比较。

Result: Mechanist能够生成更有价值的机制假设，并以更高可靠性执行实验。系统发现了跨模态安全风险，表明不安全特征可能通过表面安全的训练数据迁移；提出了关于模型信念形成与表示的机制理论，解释世界知识表示、信念形成、他人信念推断及其预训练阶段的涌现；同时将机制洞见转化为性能改进和行为控制干预，使科学基础模型生成具有指定属性的DNA序列。

Conclusion: Mechanist展示了利用智能体自动开展AI机制科学研究的可行性，并建立了从行为发现、机制解释到模型干预与控制的完整流程。该方法有望提升AI系统的可解释性、可靠性和可控性，但其结论的普适性仍需在更多模型、任务和独立实验中验证。

Abstract: AI models have achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, yet the mechanisms underlying their capabilities and the risks they may pose remain poorly understood. As AI development becomes faster and increasingly automated, mechanistic exploration remains largely manual, widening the gap between what models can do and our ability to understand and control them. To bridge this gap, we introduce Mechanist, an agentic system that uses AI as a scientific instrument for the autonomous discovery of mechanisms underlying AI intelligence. To support autonomous mechanistic discovery, we construct an interpretability-focused knowledge graph of approximately 13,000 papers and integrate it with a multidisciplinary database of 43 million papers spanning 26 fields. We further curate a library of 32 foundational methods for mechanism analysis, causal intervention, and validation. Compared with Claude Code and existing AI-scientist systems, Mechanist generates more valuable mechanism hypotheses and executes experiments more reliably. Mechanist also demonstrates a progression from discovering model behaviors to explaining and controlling AI models. Specifically, Mechanist first uncovers a counterintuitive safety risk in scientific laboratories, showing that unsafe traits can transfer across modalities through apparently safe training data. Mechanist then develops a mechanism theory of belief, revealing how models represent world knowledge, form beliefs, infer the beliefs of others, and how these mechanisms emerge during pretraining. Finally, Mechanist translates these mechanistic insights into practical interventions that improve model performance across diverse scenarios and steer scientific foundation models toward generating DNA sequences with specified properties.

</details>


### [103] [Graph-Structured Rubrics: Compiling Rubrics into Typed Evaluation Graphs for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12097)
*Xi Chen,Jie Mu,Mo Xuan,Qun Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出图结构化评分标准（GSR），将自然语言评分标准预编译为响应无关、类型明确的评价图，并通过图上的组合与输出映射完成评分或偏好判断。实验显示，GSR在多个点式评分数据集上提升了精确分数一致性，并在两个成对偏好基准上取得最高数值准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的评估器通常仅将标准作为提示上下文或扁平条件使用，虽然文本规则可能隐含了条件组合、门控和聚合逻辑，但这些结构往往未被显式建模，导致评价过程不透明、易产生组合错误且难以验证。

Method: GSR在观察待评响应前，将评分标准编译为响应无关的类型化评价图。图中包含负责生成判断的准则节点，以及通过命名端口连接的变换、归约和门控算子；图的唯一汇点经任务特定的Readout映射转换为分数或偏好结果。编译阶段会拒绝结构 malformed 或类型不兼容的图。点式评价先分别判断各个评分维度，再进行图聚合；成对评价则对每个候选响应在每项准则下分别进行一次判断，并复用同一评价图。

Result: 在GPT-OSS-120B上，相较Prometheus式评分，GSR在四个点式评分数据集上的精确分数一致性提高了0.62至6.75个百分点。在两个偏好基准上，采用原生平局与弃权策略时，GSR取得了数值上最高的端到端成对评价准确率。

Conclusion: 将评分标准显式编译为类型化评价图，可以更可靠地表达准则之间的组合关系，并提升评分一致性与偏好判断准确率。GSR同时提供了结构验证和可组合的评价流程，说明结构化评分标准是改进语言模型评估器的一种有效方向。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluators commonly treat rubrics as prompt context or flat criteria: they specify what to judge but leave criterion composition implicit, even when natural-language rules state it. We introduce Graph-Structured Rubrics (GSR), which compiles a rubric into a response-independent typed evaluation graph before observing responses. Criterion nodes elicit judgments; transformation, reduction, and gating operators compose them through named ports; and a task-specific output mapping, termed Readout, converts the unique sink into a score or preference. Compilation rejects malformed or type-incompatible graphs. Pointwise evaluation judges rubric dimensions separately before graph aggregation; pairwise evaluation reuses the graph with one judgment for each candidate under every criterion. Under GPT-OSS-120B, GSR improves exact score agreement by 0.62--6.75 percentage points over Prometheus-style scoring on four pointwise datasets and achieves the numerically highest end-to-end pairwise accuracy on two preference benchmarks under native tie and abstention policies.

</details>


### [104] [GUIDE: Governed Unified Intelligence for Document-to-Artifact Generation in Enterprise Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12133)
*Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Mohammadreza Sediqin,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出GUIDE：一个面向企业多模态指南文档的受治理多智能体框架，用于将文本、复杂表格和图像中的规则转化为可部署工件，并通过版本化规则库、结构化契约、溯源记录和人工介入降低幻觉与人工处理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业指南文档通常包含叙述文本、复杂表格和嵌入图像，现有LLM/VLM系统在此类任务中易产生内容幻觉、表格结构损失，且缺少从抽取、验证到最终工件生成的受控工作流。传统人工处理单份文档需2至3天，效率较低且难以保证一致性、可审计性与可追溯性。

Method: GUIDE采用共享的版本化规则存储库作为核心状态层，以模式验证的智能体间契约约束数据交换，并记录端到端来源信息。系统包含六类专用智能体：文档解析、基于VLM的信息抽取、一致性检查、评估、人工在环升级，以及面向不同用户角色的工件合成。该设计将自动化处理与高风险样本的人机协作结合，并强调规则、验证过程和生成结果的治理能力。

Result: 在120份真实企业指南文档上，GUIDE实现96%的文档处理成功率；共抽取3,896条规则，其中71.4%可自动批准；生成812个可部署工件；单份文档周转时间降至40至125分钟，相比原有2至3天人工流程显著缩短。摘要未说明对比基线、规则抽取的精确率与召回率、不同文档类型上的性能差异，以及人工复核后的最终质量。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，针对企业多模态文档，受治理的多智能体架构可有效连接规则抽取、质量验证、人工升级和工件生成，从而提升处理效率与可追溯性。其主要贡献在于工作流治理与端到端落地能力，而非仅提升单一抽取模型性能；但仍需通过更充分的基线比较、消融实验、错误类型分析和长期部署评估来验证其泛化性与可靠性。

Abstract: Enterprise guideline documents are heterogeneous and multimodal, combining narrative text, complex tables, and embedded images. Existing LLM and VLM systems face hallucinated content, table structure degradation, and lack governed workflows extending beyond extraction to validation and artifact generation. This leaves enterprises to perform this manually, consuming 2-3 days per document. To address this, we introduce GUIDE, a governed multi-agent framework built on a shared versioned rule store with schema-validated inter-agent contracts and end-to-end provenance tracking. Six specialized agents handle parsing, VLM-driven extraction, consistency checking, evaluation, human-in-the-loop (HITL) escalation, and persona-tailored artifact synthesis. Evaluated on 120 real-world enterprise guideline documents, GUIDE achieves 96% document success, extracts 3,896 rules with 71.4% auto-approved, produces 812 deployment-ready artifacts, and reduces turnaround to 40-125 minutes per document.

</details>


### [105] [Who Thinks Best Depends on How Long You Let Them: Budget-Dependent Rankings in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12150)
*Rodrigo Guedes de Souza,Alison R. Panisson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，语言模型的性能与模型排名会随最大生成令牌预算显著变化，固定预算的标准评测可能产生误导；预算感知路由能够利用模型互补性，但跨领域泛化有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评测通常假设模型排名在不同推理条件下保持稳定，但生成预算会影响截断、推理深度与答案质量。本文旨在检验预算变化是否导致非单调性能、排名反转及模型组合收益，并评估预算感知路由的有效性。

Method: 在64至4096令牌的七个生成预算水平下，评测四个模型在三个推理基准上的表现，共进行56,476次推理。分析非单调行为及其跨模型重合度，使用McNemar检验评估排名反转，并通过Oracle分析衡量模型互补性；进一步训练预算感知路由器，比较其跨领域与领域内性能。

Result: 3%至19%的样本出现随预算增加而准确率下降的非单调行为，且该现象具有模型特异性，跨模型重合度仅为6%至14%。所有基准上均观察到不同预算下的模型排名反转，统计显著性为p<0.01。Oracle组合最多提升27.8个百分点，且在受限预算下更明显。预算感知路由器在跨领域场景捕获了14.1%的Oracle差距；领域内加入预算特征可提升1.6至5.7个百分点，但这些特征具有领域特异性，迁移时反而下降1.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 模型评测不应只采用单一固定生成预算，而应采用预算条件化协议，同时报告不同预算下的性能与排名。预算感知路由可挖掘模型互补性，但需要谨慎处理领域偏移和预算特征的迁移能力。

Abstract: Standard evaluation of large language models assumes stable model rankings across inference conditions. We challenge this assumption by varying the token generation budget, i.e., the maximum tokens a model may produce, across seven levels (64--4,096), evaluating four models on three reasoning benchmarks (56,476 inferences). We report four findings: (i) 3--19% of items exhibit non-monotone behavior (accuracy decreasing with more budget), even after controlling for truncation, and this phenomenon is model-specific (cross-model overlap: 6--14%). (ii) Model rankings reverse across budgets on all benchmarks ($p {<} 0.01$, McNemar). (iii) Oracle analysis reveals model complementarity up to $+27.8$pp, most pronounced at constrained budgets. (iv) A budget-aware router captures 14.1% of the oracle gap cross-domain; budget features help within-domain ($+1.6$ to $+5.7$pp) but are domain-specific and hurt transfer ($-1.2$pp). These results argue for budget-conditioned evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [106] [How to Spend Your Oracle Budget: Practical Guidance for Protein Structure Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12192)
*Aleksandra Kalisz,Jack Simons,Krisztina Sinkovics,Noam Ghenassia,Shikha Surana,Henry Moss,Paul Duckworth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了蛋白质结构预测中多种受外部评估器指导的方法，发现方法优劣取决于评估器查询预算：低预算下O3最有效，高预算下FK-steering和DPO表现更佳，不存在全场景占优的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在部分蛋白质目标上的结构预测仍不可靠，而能够识别或修正错误的生物学评估器成本较高。因此，如何在有限评估器预算下选择FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N采样或O3等指导策略，缺乏系统性的实证依据。

Method: 研究将FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N采样与O3进行基准比较，并将原本在生成模型潜空间中使用现成优化器的O3扩展到蛋白质结构预测任务。实验在钙调蛋白1CLL和大肠杆菌天冬氨酸转氨甲酰酶9EEH两个目标上开展，考察不同评估器类型与不同查询预算下的方法性能。

Result: 实验表明，各方法的性能随评估器预算和所用评估器而变化，没有任何单一方法在全部条件下稳定领先。O3在低查询预算条件下最具效果；随着预算增加，FK-steering和DPO的性能改善并展现出更强竞争力。

Conclusion: 评估器预算应被视为蛋白质结构预测指导方法选择的核心约束。实践中，预算有限时宜优先考虑O3；拥有较高查询预算时，FK-steering或DPO可能更合适。该研究为预算敏感的评估器指导提供了实用的比较参考，但其结论仍需在更多蛋白质目标和评估器上进一步验证。

Abstract: Foundation models for protein structure prediction remain unreliable on certain targets. External oracles can flag and correct these failures, but biological oracles are expensive, making oracle budget a critical constraint. Existing guidance methods, such as FK-steering, DPO, and Best K-of-N sampling, differ in how they spend this budget, yet no systematic comparison exists to guide method selection. To bridge this gap, we benchmark these methods alongside the recently proposed Optimisation Over Outputs (O3), which applies off-the-shelf optimisers within a generative model's latent subspace. We extend the usage of O3 to protein structure prediction models. Overall, our work provides the first practical reference for oracle budget-aware guidance. Our evaluation on two protein targets, calmodulin (1CLL) and E. coli aspartate transcarbamoylase (9EEH), reveals that no single method consistently dominates across all budgets and oracles. Specifically, O3 proves most effective at low oracle budgets, while FK-steering and DPO demonstrate improved performance as the budget increases. We distil these findings into actionable recommendations for practitioners operating under real-world oracle-budget constraints.

</details>


### [107] [An Agentic Workflow for Legacy HPC Modernization: Converting the Two-Electron-Integral Core of GAMESS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12249)
*Yuzhong Shen,Masha Sosonkina,Peng Xu,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向大规模遗留 Fortran 现代化的智能体工作流，并在 GAMESS 的关键积分例程上验证：将 12 个固定格式 Fortran 77 文件迁移至自由格式 Fortran 2008 后，全部通过严格测试，未发现任何与计算结果相关的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留 Fortran 代码库规模庞大，单项迁移操作虽常规，但人工改造成本高，导致大量科学计算软件长期未现代化。论文旨在评估：在保留少量人工审批环节、并依赖领域精确验证机制的前提下，智能体能够安全承担多大范围的生产级代码迁移工作。

Method: 作者设计了由三个提示词专门化智能体角色构成的协作流程。智能体在隔离的版本控制工作区中执行任务，并共同编写和迭代版本化规范；人工仅负责少数关键门控。案例对象为 GAMESS 中用于计算双电子积分的例程，覆盖 12 个源文件、56,448 行代码和 225 个子程序。迁移目标是从固定格式 Fortran 77 转换为自由格式 Fortran 2008。安全性通过领域既有的精确验证预言机保障：以标准测试产生的能量输出逐位一致作为合并标准。

Result: 迁移后的全部 12 个文件通过由 49 个标准 GAMESS 测试和 2 个补充计算组成的 51 项验证。累计 612 次测试运行中，计算结果相关差异为零；即使第 12 位小数的偏差也会判定为失败。此外，所有文件均通过项目持续集成所使用的 Jenkins 测试。智能体工作跨越四代 Claude 模型完成。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，对于拥有强、精确且可自动执行验证预言机的遗留科学软件，智能体可在有限人工监督下完成大规模、生产级的代码现代化，并保持可验证的行为等价性。其安全委托边界取决于验证机制的覆盖范围：验证预言机无法观测或判定的行为，仍不能被同等程度地安全委托。

Abstract: Modernizing legacy Fortran is a problem of volume: the transformations are individually routine, but the codebases can be enormous, and across much of computational science the work simply goes undone. We propose an agentic workflow that takes this work on at production scale, and we set out to measure how far such delegation can reach. In this work, three prompt-specialized agent roles operate under a version-controlled specification that the agents themselves authored and revised, while humans hold a small number of gates. The arrangement is kept safe by an exact verification oracle inherited from the domain, and the boundary of safe delegation lies exactly where that oracle stops seeing.
  We apply the proposed workflow in a case study, converting the two-electron-integral routines of GAMESS (General Atomic and Molecular Electronic Structure System), a mature quantum-chemistry package with a 48-year development history, from fixed-form Fortran 77 to free-form Fortran 2008. The scope of this work was twelve source files, 56,448 lines, and 225 subroutines for computing electron repulsion integrals. The agents ran as three Claude Code roles in isolated worktrees, and the work spanned four Claude model generations. Because the GAMESS group ships a standard test suite whose printed energies its user community treats as canonical, we could adopt bit-for-bit reproduction of those energies as the merge criterion, where a deviation in the twelfth decimal place counts as a failure rather than drift. All twelve source files pass a 51-test validation battery comprising the 49 standard GAMESS tests and two additional calculations, and across 612 test runs the number of chemistry-relevant differences is zero, and every file also passes the Jenkins tests that are used for continuous integration.

</details>


### [108] [VAKRA: Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Across APIs and Retrieval Under Tool-Use Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12282)
*Ankita Rajaram Naik,Anupama Murthi,Benjamin Elder,Siyu Huo,Raavi Gupta,Abhinav Jain,Praveen Venkateswaran,Abdulhamid Adebayo,Danish Contractor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VAKRA 是一个面向企业智能体的综合评测基准，用于测试模型结合结构化 API 与文档知识库进行检索、工具调用和多跳推理的能力。实验显示，即使最强模型在复杂组合与策略约束场景下也存在明显性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准通常将 API 调用、知识检索和复杂推理分开评估，难以反映企业环境中智能体需要跨越多个数据源、遵守自然语言工具使用规则并完成端到端任务的真实需求。因此，作者希望建立统一且可执行的评测框架，识别模型能力短板。

Method: 作者构建 VAKRA 基准，覆盖 62 个领域、超过 8,000 个可执行 API，并设置三类难度递增任务：多样化 API 交互形式、基于结构化 API 的多跳推理，以及同时结合多源信息与自然语言工具使用策略约束的推理。评测时采用固定的 ReAct 智能体框架，以降低不同智能体架构造成的干扰；通过重新执行模型预测的工具调用来验证正确性，并允许存在多条有效求解路径。

Result: 最佳模型在单跳、端点式 API 任务上的准确率仅为 70.4%，在组合式 API 任务中降至约 50%至51%。随着推理深度增加，性能下降超过 50%。在含有工具策略约束的任务中，模型尤其脆弱：对于不可回答问题，最低表现仅为 2.4%。轨迹分析表明，主要错误并非来自 API 调用语法或执行机制，而是集中于由自然语言驱动的推理环节，包括实体消歧、跨来源信息对齐与证据落地。

Conclusion: VAKRA 表明，当前前沿与开源模型尚未具备可靠的企业级 API—知识库联合推理能力。未来改进重点应从单纯提升工具调用格式正确性，转向增强实体解析、跨源证据整合、组合式规划，以及对自然语言工具使用约束和不可回答情形的稳健处理。

Abstract: Agents deployed in enterprise settings must reason across structured APIs and document collections, yet existing benchmarks evaluate these capabilities in isolation. We introduce VAKRA (e\textbf{V}aluating \textbf{A}PI and \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{R}etrieval \textbf{A}gents), a benchmark of over $8{,}000$ executable APIs across $62$ domains with tasks spanning three settings of increasing difficulty: diverse API interaction styles, multi-hop reasoning over structured APIs, and multi-source reasoning with natural-language tool-use policy constraints. Correctness is verified by re-executing predicted tool calls against live APIs, accommodating multiple valid paths. Using a fixed ReAct harness to isolate model capabilities from agent architecture, we evaluate frontier and open-weight models and find that even the best model achieves only 70.4\% on single-hop endpoint-style tasks and drops to 50--51\% on compositional APIs; performance degrades by over 50\% as reasoning depth increases, and policy-constrained questions expose severe failures (as low as 2.4\% on unanswerable queries). Trace analysis shows failures concentrate at language-mediated reasoning - entity disambiguation, cross-source grounding, rather than tool invocation mechanics. Code is available https://github.com/IBM/VAKRA. Dataset is available https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-research/VAKRA

</details>


### [109] [Constructing Dynamic Master Logic Models as Knowledge Graphs for Complex System Diagnostics Using Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12304)
*Saman Marandi,Yu-Shu Hu,Mohammad Modarres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用检索增强生成与大语言模型，从系统技术文档自动构建动态主逻辑知识图谱（KG-DML）的框架，并在较大规模复杂系统上验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态主逻辑模型主要依赖专家人工解读文档并建立功能—结构映射，过程成本高、可扩展性有限，难以适应复杂系统的建模、诊断与可靠性分析需求。

Method: 框架依据动态主逻辑的层级结构，采用面向各层级的定向检索与大语言模型抽取，从系统描述中识别功能目标、结构元素、功能依赖及显式逻辑关系，并将其表示为知识图谱。验证方法包括分层精确率与召回率评估、逻辑门一致性检查及整体结构完整性评价；同时通过重复运行检验模型重建的一致性。

Result: 在一个退役沸水反应堆的低压冷却剂注入系统案例中，该方法能够较稳定地重建KG-DML模型。生成的模型保留了层级功能依赖和逻辑门关系，并支持故障向上传播、依赖关系向下追溯、诊断推理与安全性评估。

Conclusion: 检索增强生成和大语言模型可有效支持从技术文档自动生成可执行的动态主逻辑知识图谱，为复杂工程系统的功能建模、故障诊断及可靠性分析提供了可扩展的方法，但其实际应用仍需结合分层准确性、逻辑一致性和结构完整性进行严格验证。

Abstract: Dynamic Master Logic (DML) provides a hierarchical framework for representing system behavior by linking functional objectives to underlying structural elements. However, DML construction typically relies on expert interpretation of technical documentation, limiting scalability for complex systems. This study presents a framework for automated construction of DML models from system descriptions and their representation as Knowledge Graphs (KG-DML), using Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Large Language Models as enabling tools. Building on prior work with small-scale systems, the framework extends automated KG-DML construction and evaluation to substantially larger and more complex systems. Model construction proceeds across the DML hierarchy using targeted retrieval while preserving functional dependencies and explicit logical relationships. The resulting KG-DML supports diagnostic reasoning, safety assessment, upward failure propagation, and downward dependency tracing. A multi-level validation methodology evaluates layer-specific precision and recall, logical gate consistency, and overall structural integrity. Application to the Low-Pressure Coolant Injection system of a decommissioned Boiling Water Reactor demonstrates consistent reconstruction across repeated runs. The results show that automated KG-DML construction can transform technical documentation into executable functional models for diagnostic and reliability analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [MergirafSemi: A Language-Agnostic Semistructured Merge Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11345)
*Pedro Lopes,Paulo Borba,Paola Accioly,Guilherme Cavalcanti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出MergirafSemi，一种语言无关的半结构化代码合并工具。它利用轻量级具体语法树识别程序结构区域，并在区域内部采用基于行的合并，以在冲突检测准确性、漏检风险、跨语言适用性和运行效率之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于文本行的合并工具容易产生伪冲突，也可能遗漏真实冲突；完全结构化工具虽然能利用语法信息提升自动合并能力，但计算开销较高，且通常需要面向特定语言开发和维护。本文旨在降低结构化合并的工程与性能成本，同时保留结构感知带来的收益。

Method: MergirafSemi使用轻量级具体语法树而非完整的语言专属语义模型，定位方法体等程序结构区域，并据此指导合并策略。在这些区域内仍采用行级合并，从而避免构建复杂的完整结构化表示。论文在多种编程语言的真实合并场景中，将其与非结构化、语言专属半结构化及结构化工具进行经验比较。

Result: 实验表明，结构粒度提升通常能够减少需人工处理的冲突并提高自动合并比例，但更激进的合并决策也会增加遗漏真实冲突的风险。MergirafSemi相较非结构化工具显著减少伪冲突；相较语言专属半结构化工具，其合并效果相当，但执行更稳健，并在多数常见场景下具有更低的运行时间开销。

Conclusion: MergirafSemi证明了语言无关的半结构化合并能够提供实用折中：它以较低实现和维护成本利用程序结构信息，减少伪冲突，并避免完全结构化方法可能带来的高开销与过度激进合并。论文的核心启示是，合并工具不应仅追求更细粒度的结构表示，还需综合控制真实冲突漏检、效率和跨语言可移植性。

Abstract: Developers frequently face merge conflicts when integrating concurrent changes. Most merge tools rely on unstructured, line-based comparisons, often producing spurious conflicts and missing actual conflicts. To address these limitations, structure-aware merge tools have been proposed, which leverage syntactic representations to improve merge accuracy. However, fully structured tools may incur higher computational cost, and language-specific tools require significant development and maintenance effort. To balance these trade-offs, we propose MergirafSemi, a language-agnostic semistructured merge tool that captures structural information without requiring full structural modeling or language-specific implementations, and applies line-based merging within specific program regions, such as method bodies in Java. Our tool leverages lightweight Concrete Syntax Trees to guide merging decisions while preserving flexibility and efficiency across languages. We evaluate our tool through an empirical study on real-world merge scenarios across multiple programming languages, comparing it with unstructured, semistructured, and structured tools. Our results show that increasing structural granularity improves automatic conflict resolution but can also lead to more aggressive merge decisions, increasing the number of missed actual conflicts. In contrast, MergirafSemi achieves a more balanced trade-off, reducing spurious conflicts while maintaining competitive accuracy and better runtime performance in most common scenarios. Compared to an unstructured tool, it substantially reduces spurious conflicts, and when compared to a semistructured language-specific tool, it achieves comparable effectiveness while exhibiting more robust execution behavior and significantly lower runtime overhead.

</details>


### [111] [The Devil Is in the Interface: Evaluating How Tool Architecture Shapes Coding Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11386)
*Xiangzhe Xu,Hamidreza Saghir,Qianhui Wu,Marc-Alexandre Côté,Tong Wang,Kiran Lakkaraju,Kexin Pei,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过11,700条轨迹的受控实验表明：在能力基本相同的前提下，工具架构——即工具如何被组织并暴露给模型——会显著改变代码智能体的行为、效率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有智能体工具研究主要关注增加可执行能力，而较少系统研究这些能力的接口组织方式。作者希望分离“工具能力”与“工具架构”的影响，明确何种工具暴露形式更有利于仓库级代码问题修复。

Method: 研究在仓库级Issue修复任务上比较六种工具架构，尽量保持底层可访问的信息与可执行操作相近，仅改变其组织和交互方式。实验覆盖三个智能体执行主体，并统计总计11,700条执行轨迹；比较对象包括仅提供Bash的基础架构、结构化低层接口、自然语言搜索接口、Python CodeAct风格接口，以及提供中间推理记录的轻量级文本认知支架工具。

Result: 相较于仅有Bash工具的基础架构，结构化低层接口可将重复尝试间的一致性最高提升4.7倍；自然语言搜索可扩大仓库探索范围，并使智能体访问相关文件的比例提高超过11%；Python CodeAct风格接口在维持相近任务表现的同时，将执行步数减少41.6%、令牌消耗降低56.3%。相反，允许记录中间推理等轻量级文本认知支架工具对智能体行为影响有限。

Conclusion: 工具设计不应仅关注增加功能集合，还应将工具架构视为核心设计变量。对于代码智能体，结构化接口、面向语义的搜索与可组合的代码执行接口能够分别改善稳定性、信息检索覆盖和交互效率；简单的文本推理记录机制则未显示出明显收益。

Abstract: As large language models continue to improve, agentic systems are becoming increasingly important, and tools are a key design dimension because they determine how agents access information and take action in their environments. Prior work on agent tooling has primarily focused on expanding what agents can do, but has paid less systematic attention to how those capabilities are organized and exposed to the model. We refer to this latter design dimension as tool architecture. We study tool architecture in coding agents through controlled experiments on repository-level issue fixing, comparing six tool architectures that hold the underlying information and actions similar while varying how they are organized and exposed to the model, across three actors and a total of 11,700 trajectories. Our experiments show that, even when tools provide similar capabilities, tool architecture changes agent behavior: Compared to a basic architecture where the agent has only the bash tool, more structured low-level interfaces improve consistency across repeated attempts by up to 4.7 $\times$; natural-language search broadens repository exploration and increases access to relevant files by more than 11%; and Python CodeAct-style interfaces achieve similar task performance with 41.6% fewer steps and 56.3% lower token usage. By contrast, lightweight text-based cognitive-scaffolding tools, such as tools that let the agent record intermediate reasoning, have limited effect on actor behavior.

</details>


### [112] [GraphAlignCoder: Aligning Program and Proof Graphs for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11394)
*Yueke Zhang,Zihan Fang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GraphAlignCoder通过将程序实现图与Lean形式化证明流图引入代码大模型训练，使模型不仅学习代码是否通过执行，还学习各代码区域为何满足正确性约束，从而显著提升复杂编程任务的求解能力与跨基准泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于执行反馈的代码训练通常只提供程序最终成功或失败的信号，监督较粗粒度，难以揭示正确程序应具备的控制、依赖及语义组织结构。因此，模型可能生成语法合理、可局部运行但违反隐含语义约束的代码。该工作旨在将显式的正确性结构转化为可学习监督，以增强模型的程序推理与可靠生成能力。

Method: 该框架包含两类结构化信号：第一，从候选实现中构建实现图，用于表示不同程序区域之间的控制关系和依赖关系；第二，通过受约束的Lean证明流水线生成证明轨迹，并提取形式化证明流图。训练分为两个阶段：先联合学习可执行代码及基于图的区域级正确性描述，使模型获得对局部正确性依据的理解；再将这些验证知识整合回纯代码生成能力，即验证到代码的知识巩固。

Result: GraphAlignCoder在所有报告基准上均优于基础模型、仅代码监督微调和CodeRL。相较CodeRL，其在LiveCodeBench v6上的解决数量由38提升至50，绝对增加12题、相对提升31.6%；在BigCodeBench Hard上由16提升至23，绝对增加7题、相对提升43.8%；在BigCodeBench Full上由359提升至363。消融实验表明，验证图注入带来初始推理增益，而将验证知识巩固至代码生成阶段则是获得稳定跨基准迁移能力的关键。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，代码大模型训练可从仅依赖结果反馈转向学习显式、区域级的正确性结构。实现图和形式化证明流图为模型提供了比执行成败更细致的语义监督；其中，图注入改善推理过程，验证知识向代码生成的迁移保证泛化效果。该方法的主要贡献是建立了形式化验证结构与代码生成训练之间的桥梁，并在高难度代码基准上取得实证提升。

Abstract: Code large language models (LLMs) can generate syntactically plausible programs that nevertheless violate hidden semantic constraints. Existing execution-feedback training methods identify whether a completed program fails, but provide limited supervision about how a correct solution should be organized. We introduce GraphAlignCoder, a training framework that transfers explicit correctness structure into code generation.
  GraphAlignCoder constructs an implementation graph that captures control and dependence among program regions. In parallel, a constrained Lean pipeline produces proof traces, from which we extract a formal proof-flow graph. The model first learns executable code together with graph-derived descriptions of why individual program regions are correct, and then consolidates this knowledge into code generation. GraphAlignCoder consistently outperforms the base model, code-only SFT, and CodeRL across all benchmarks. Compared with CodeRL, it increases the solved count from 38 to 50 on LiveCodeBench v6 and from 16 to 23 on BigCodeBench Hard, corresponding to relative gains of 31.6% and 43.8%, while also improving BigCodeBench Full from 359 to 363 tasks. The ablation study further shows that verification-graph injection produces the initial reasoning gain, while verification to code consolidation is essential for robust cross-benchmark transfer.

</details>


### [113] [Simplifying Requirements Engineering in the Context of the LGPD: An LLM-Based Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11454)
*Cinara Gomes de Melo Carneiro,Renato de Freitas Bulcão Neto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究探讨利用大语言模型将《巴西通用数据保护法》中的法律规范自动转化为软件需求工件，以支持隐私合规的软件开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私法规的法律条文难以直接映射为可实施、可验证的软件需求，传统需求工程在法规解释、需求提取和测试设计方面成本较高且易产生偏差。

Method: 研究基于现行隐私立法，构建使用大语言模型自动生成用户故事和验收测试场景的方法，并通过性能评估检验其生成结果对法规合规需求表达的有效性。

Result: 评估显示该方法具有较高表现，表明大语言模型能够较有效地生成与隐私法规相关的需求和验收测试工件。摘要未提供具体模型、数据集、评估指标、对比基线或量化结果。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在将隐私法律规范前置融入软件需求工程方面具有应用潜力，可帮助团队从开发初期识别和落实合规要求；但其实际可靠性仍需通过可追溯性、法律专家审查、错误率及跨法规泛化能力等进一步验证。

Abstract: Compliance with privacy legislation poses a complex challenge to Requirements Engineering (RE): translating legal norms into software requirements. In this context, this study investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can simplify RE within the framework of the Brazilian General Data Protection Law (LGPD). The proposed approach utilizes current legislation to automatically generate User Stories and Acceptance Test Scenarios. The evaluation results demonstrated high performance, confirming the potential of LLMs to ensure regulatory compliance from the software's inception.

</details>


### [114] [Do Influence Tactics Matter? Investigating Prompt Framing Effects in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11513)
*Alex Deaconu,Anubhav Gupta,Manaal Basha,Nicholas Haydu,Gema Rodríguez-Pérez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究考察心理学中的人际影响策略如何改变编程提示词，并评估其对开源大语言模型代码生成表现的影响。结果表明，尤其是带有紧迫性暗示的提示框架，可能降低代码正确性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已确认提示词措辞和结构会影响大语言模型表现，但较少系统研究源自人类沟通与说服理论的提示框架，是否会在软件工程任务中稳定地影响模型行为及代码质量。

Method: 研究基于Yukl与Falbe的影响策略分类法，将八类影响策略操作化为可复用的提示词模板；随后在五个主流开源权重大语言模型上，使用LiveCodeBench和SWE-bench Verified两个基准进行评测，并从功能正确性、代码质量、可维护性和安全性四个维度分析输出结果。

Result: 不同心理影响策略对应的提示框架会造成可观察的性能差异。研究发现，某些强调紧迫性或催促意味的框架与较低的功能正确性和安全性相关，说明语言中的社会心理线索可能干扰模型在编程任务中的可靠输出。

Conclusion: 提示词设计不应仅关注任务描述的完整性，也应审慎处理带有情绪、压力或说服色彩的表达。该研究为代码生成场景中更透明、可解释的人机交互设计提供了经验依据，但摘要未说明具体效应量、统计显著性及不同模型间的一致性，相关结论仍需结合全文实验细节进一步判断。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software engineering workflows, helping developers write, debug, test, and maintain code. While prompt wording and structure are known to influence model performance, the impact of psychologically inspired prompt framings remains unexplored. This study investigates whether different psychology-based communication strategies that humans use to persuade or motivate others can lead to more effective prompt framing, which may, in turn, affect LLM behaviour in coding tasks. Drawing on Yukl & Falbe's well-known taxonomy, we operationalized eight influence tactics (like rational persuasion, ingratiation, and exchange) into reproducible prompt templates. These prompt templates were evaluated across five leading open-weight LLMs using two widely adopted benchmarks: LiveCodeBench and SWE-bench Verified. We assessed the resulting code output on four key software quality dimensions: functional correctness, quality, maintainability, and security. Our results show that certain influence-induced prompt framings, particularly those emphasizing urgency, were associated with reduced correctness and security. This work presents the first large-scale empirical study of influence-induced prompt framing in software engineering tasks, offering insights into how linguistic cues may shape LLM outputs. We conclude with practical insights for designing transparent and interpretable human-AI interactions in code generation.

</details>


### [115] [Harnessing LLMs for Document-Guided Fuzzing of Python Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11744)
*Bin Duan,Tarek Mahmud,Meiru Che,Yan Yan,Naipeng Dong,Dan Dongseong Kim,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VistaFuzz是一种文档引导的模糊测试技术，利用本地部署的开源大语言模型从Python API文档中提取参数约束及参数间依赖关系，并生成有效测试输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python库广泛应用于深度学习、科学计算、数据分析和计算机视觉，其API可靠性至关重要。现有测试方法通常忽略约束，或依赖特定库的解析规则，难以处理参数之间的复杂依赖。

Method: VistaFuzz通过本地部署的开源大语言模型解析API文档，提取单参数约束和参数间关系，并据此生成满足联合约束的模糊测试输入。研究在12个Python库的7,718个API上进行了评估。

Result: 在测试API中，40.1%存在参数间关系。禁用参数依赖解析后，这些API的有效输入生成率由95%以上下降至31.6%—52.8%。VistaFuzz共发现74个问题，其中43个已获开发者确认，29个已修复。

Conclusion: 参数间依赖是Python API有效模糊测试的重要因素。VistaFuzz能够利用API文档自动恢复此类约束，并显著提高有效输入生成能力，同时发现具有实际影响的软件缺陷。

Abstract: Python libraries underpin deep learning, scientific computing, data analysis, and computer vision, making their reliability critical to downstream applications. Testing their APIs requires inputs that satisfy both per-parameter constraints and dependencies among parameters. Existing approaches either leave such constraints implicit in generated programs or rely on library-specific parsing rules. This paper introduces VistaFuzz, a document-guided fuzzing technique that uses a locally served open-sourced LLM to extract parameter specifications from API documents and generate inputs that satisfy both parameter constraints and inter-parameter dependencies. We evaluate VistaFuzz on 7,718 APIs across twelve Python libraries. Inter-parameter relationships occur in 40.1\% of tested APIs, and disabling their resolution reduces the valid generation rate on those APIs from above 95\% to 31.6\%--52.8\%. VistaFuzz reports 74 issues, of which 43 have been confirmed by developers and 29 have been fixed.

</details>


### [116] [Instruction Alignment for Binary Code Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11766)
*Huaijin Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出将指令级对齐监督引入二进制代码表征学习，以弥补现有方法仅关注函数级粗粒度语义、忽略细粒度指令对应关系的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制函数嵌入方法主要优化函数级相似性，难以充分利用编译调试信息中可获得的指令对应关系，因而限制了表征的精确性与可解释性。预实验还发现，经过函数级相似性微调的模型具有更好的指令对齐能力，说明二者存在较强关联。

Method: 作者将指令对齐作为辅助训练目标，与函数级表征学习联合优化。训练监督来自能够建立指令对应关系的调试信息；模型除学习函数整体相似性外，还被要求识别或强化跨二进制函数的细粒度指令匹配关系。

Result: 实验表明，加入指令对齐训练后，模型在检索准确率上得到提升；同时，模型进行相似性判断时获得了更具区分性的证据，表明细粒度监督改善了嵌入空间的判别能力。

Conclusion: 指令级对齐是提升二进制代码表征质量的有效辅助信号。该工作表明，函数级语义学习与指令级对应学习可以相互促进，联合训练有助于获得更准确、可解释且适用于相似函数检索的二进制代码表示。

Abstract: Binary code representation learning is a fundamental problem in software security and reverse engineering. Existing methods mainly learn function-level embeddings that capture coarse-grained semantic relationships between binary functions, but they largely ignore fine-grained instruction-level correspondences. This limitation misses valuable supervision signals available from compiler debug information, which can support the learning of more accurate and interpretable binary code representations.
  We propose to leverage instruction alignment knowledge to further improve binary code representation learning. Our preliminary study reveals that models finetuned for function-level binary code similarity exhibit substantially better instruction alignment than their pre-trained model, suggesting a strong correlation between instruction alignment and function-level embedding quality. Motivated by this observation, we design a training approach that explicitly incorporates instruction alignment as an auxiliary training objective. Our experiments show that instruction alignment training improves retrieval accuracy and provides more discriminative signal for the model's similarity judgments.

</details>


### [117] [Testing Deep Learning Library APIs via Cross-Framework Differential Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11886)
*Bin Duan,Ruican Dong,Naipeng Dong,Dan Dongseong Kim,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Xamt，一种面向深度学习库API的跨框架差分模糊测试方法。它在7个库中构建等价API组，并通过差分执行发现崩溃和输出不一致问题，共复现72个缺陷案例，其中25个已获开发者确认、23个已修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习库API测试通常依赖库内性质或CPU/GPU差分测试，难以发现那些在不同硬件后端上表现一致、但在不同框架实现之间存在差异的缺陷。因此，需要一种能够比较多个深度学习框架等价API行为的测试方法。

Method: Xamt首先利用显式API别名与参数角色归一化建立跨框架API候选对应关系；随后通过成对执行及组级行为检查，在规范化普通输入上验证这些API是否确实等价。对验证后的API组，Xamt采用方差引导的差分模糊测试，生成普通、边界和非有限数值输入。其测试预言机检测异常终止，以及不同框架间输出不一致的情况，并将可疑案例提交复现和人工分析。

Result: Xamt在7个深度学习库中构建了676个经执行验证的等价API组，覆盖2,563个匹配API。测试发现并独立复现72个差异案例，包括4个崩溃案例和68个输出不一致案例。在提交给开发者的72份报告中，25份已被确认，其中23个问题已修复。

Conclusion: 跨框架差分测试能够补足库内性质测试和CPU/GPU差分测试的覆盖盲区。Xamt通过自动构建、验证和模糊测试跨库等价API组，有效发现了大量具有实际修复价值的实现差异与崩溃缺陷，表明其对提升深度学习基础库可靠性具有显著作用。

Abstract: Deep learning libraries underpin many safety- and reliability-critical applications, yet existing API-level testing techniques often rely on intra-library properties or CPU--GPU differential oracles and may miss defects that behave consistently across hardware backends. We present Xamt, a cross-framework differential fuzzing approach for deep learning library APIs. Xamt constructs and tests execution-validated groups of APIs intended to implement equivalent operations across seven libraries. It uses explicit API aliases and parameter-role normalization to construct candidate correspondences and validates them through pairwise execution and a group-level behavioral check on canonical ordinary inputs. The resulting groups are explored using variance-guided differential fuzzing with ordinary, boundary, and non-finite inputs. Crash and inconsistency oracles flag executions exhibiting abnormal termination or inconsistent outputs for subsequent reproduction and analysis. Across the seven libraries, Xamt constructs 676 execution-validated groups containing 2,563 matched APIs. Among these, Xamt identifies 72 independently reproduced discrepancy cases, including 4 crash cases and 68 output inconsistencies. Among the 72 developer reports, 25 have been confirmed, including 23 that have been fixed.

</details>


### [118] [Developing LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering: A Mixed-Method Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11965)
*Mariama Celi Serafim De Oliveira,Motunrayo Osatohanmen Ibiyo,Marco Gianrusso,Claudio Di Sipio,Davide Di Ruscio,Phuong T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了面向软件工程的多智能体系统框架，通过文档、功能与能力的定量比较，以及README文件摘要任务的定性实验，为开发者选择合适框架提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一大语言模型已被用于代码生成、调试、测试和维护等任务，但多智能体系统需要开发者处理技术选型、角色设计和协同规则制定等复杂问题。现有框架众多，缺少从开发者视角出发的综合比较与实证评估。

Method: 研究分为两部分：首先，对具有代表性的开源多智能体框架开展定量分析，考察其文档质量、功能集合和能力支持；其次，选取部分框架实现统一的README.MD文件摘要用例，进行定性和经验性比较，并以ROUGE指标评估摘要结果。

Result: 多数被选框架能够较充分覆盖多智能体系统的基础组成部分，包括智能体组织、任务协作及流程编排等。但高级能力仍不完善，尤其是智能体遥测与运行观测支持不足。在README摘要实验中，不同框架的ROUGE得分不存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 框架之间在该摘要任务上的最终文本质量差异有限，因此选型不应仅依赖ROUGE等任务指标。开发者和研究者应结合文档成熟度、基础功能覆盖、协同机制、可观测性及具体项目需求进行选择；智能体遥测等高级工程化能力是后续框架发展的重要方向。

Abstract: The proliferation of Generative Artificial Intelligence (Gen AI) powered by large language models (LLMs) has transformed the software development process, introducing new paradigms for code generation, debugging, testing, and maintenance. While early applications focused on leveraging single, independent LLMs to assist developers with isolated tasks, recent advances have shifted toward multi-agent systems (MAS) that orchestrate multiple LLM-based agents working collaboratively toward common objectives. Despite their promising potential, using MAS encompasses a set of challenges for developers who have to carefully select the right technology, devise proper coordination rules, and design specific roles for the involved agents. In this paper, we provide a comprehensive overview of the existing tools and frameworks for implementing MAS in software engineering. First, we conducted a quantitative analysis of the most relevant open source MAS frameworks by evaluating their documentation, features, and capabilities from the developers' perspective. Second, we performed a qualitative evaluation of a subset of the selected frameworks by implementing a common use case: the summarization of README.MD files. The findings show that the selected frameworks provide a good coverage of fundamental components of MAS, though advanced features such as telemetry of agents are still missing. In addition, the empirical evaluation shows that there is no significant difference in terms of ROUGE scores considering the summarization task. Finally, we provide a set of lessons learned and challenges that can help researchers and practitioners to select a suitable MAS framework according to their needs.

</details>


### [119] [RealisticTritonBench: A Benchmark for Triton-Kernel Generation in Real-World AI Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12004)
*Jinjun Huang,Zhongzhen Wen,Tongtong Xu,Meng Yan,Xin Xia,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出RealisticTritonBench：一个从主流AI框架真实代码贡献中提取任务的Triton内核生成基准，用端到端框架测试替代仅评估孤立内核的方式，以更真实地衡量大语言模型生成Triton内核的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Triton代码生成评测存在三项不足：任务大多局限于PyTorch到Triton的翻译，覆盖不了实际工程任务的多样性；只测单个内核性能，不能反映框架集成后的端到端收益；依赖人工编写的单内核评测脚本，可能存在可被模型利用的正确性验证缺陷，导致评分虚高。

Method: 作者从流行开源AI框架中系统收集修改Triton内核的真实代码贡献，并将其转化为带有具体工程上下文的生成任务。模型根据自然语言需求实现相应Triton内核；随后，生成结果被集成回原始框架，在完整、可复现的环境中通过端到端测试验证正确性与实际性能。

Result: 对多种领先大语言模型的实验表明，即使模型已能在部分受控场景中生成Triton代码，它们在来源于真实工程的Triton内核生成任务上仍存在明显困难。该结果说明传统基准可能高估了模型的实际部署能力。

Conclusion: RealisticTritonBench通过真实工程来源、完整上下文和端到端评估，提升了Triton内核生成评测的真实性与可信度。论文的核心结论是：当前大语言模型距离可靠完成生产级Triton内核开发仍有较大差距，后续研究应重视复杂工程约束、框架集成正确性与端到端性能。

Abstract: In modern AI frameworks, GPU kernels are key to overall system performance. Combining usability, portability, and near-handwritten CUDA performance, Triton is widely adopted for implementing GPU kernels. Recent advances show the potential of large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate Triton kernels, reducing the manual effort required from expert kernel developers. Several benchmarks evaluate LLM-generated Triton kernels. However, they suffer from three key limitations: (1) they restrict tasks to PyTorch-to-Triton translation, failing to reflect the diversity and complexity of real-world Triton tasks; (2) they evaluate only individual-kernel performance rather than end-to-end performance, the core criterion for real-world deployment in AI frameworks; and (3) they rely on manually written evaluation scripts for individual kernels, which may contain flaws that models can exploit to bypass correctness checks and obtain inflated scores. To address these limitations, we introduce RealisticTritonBench, the first benchmark to derive Triton kernel generation tasks from real-world pull requests in popular AI frameworks, enabling realistic, production-like evaluation. RealisticTritonBench systematically extracts PRs that modify Triton kernels from popular open-source AI frameworks and transforms them into generation tasks with concrete engineering contexts. Each task takes a natural language requirement as input and requires a corresponding Triton kernel implementation, with a complete and reproducible evaluation environment. Unlike prior benchmarks focused on isolated kernel performance, RealisticTritonBench integrates generated kernels into their original frameworks and evaluates them using end-to-end tests, enabling a more faithful assessment. We evaluate leading LLMs on RealisticTritonBench and find that they still struggle with real-world Triton kernel generation tasks.

</details>


### [120] [From Safety Documentation to Safety Knowledge Support: An Evidence-Grounded LLM Framework for Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12025)
*Tuhinangshu Gangopadhyay,Rasmus Adler,Peter Liggesmeyer,Jan Reich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向医疗器械安全工程的“证据驱动型安全知识支持”框架，利用大语言模型辅助整理、关联、核查和更新安全工件，但明确保留专家对安全结论的最终决策权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗器械的软件化、互联化和智能化提高了风险管理复杂度。ISO 14971与IEC 62304要求风险相关证据在需求、设计、软件变更、验证、投诉及上市后数据之间保持一致，但这类工作成本高且依赖稀缺的安全与领域专家。现有研究多聚焦孤立任务，缺少来源链接、可追溯性、不确定性处理、生命周期更新和专家审查记录，因此难以直接用于受监管开发环境。

Method: 作者将研究重点从“生成安全文本”转向“支持来源可追溯的安全知识”。所提框架连接医疗器械开发工件、受控知识存储与检索、面向具体方法的候选安全项生成、批判性审查与不确定性检查，以及可记录的专家复核。框架旨在为专家准备、关联、检查和更新候选安全工件，而非自动判定器械安全性或替代监管审批。

Result: 摘要未报告实证实验结果，而是提出了框架及评估方案。拟采用非公开或新建的医疗器械案例，并以专家参考分析作为对照，从覆盖度、正确性、相关性、可追溯性、重复率、无证据支撑主张数量及专家审查工作量等维度进行评估。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是界定了医疗器械领域LLM安全辅助的关键问题：应构建可追溯、证据受控、可处理不确定性且保留专家闭环的安全知识支持系统，而不是仅追求自动生成安全文档。该定位更符合受监管医疗器械开发中的审计、变更管理和责任分配要求。

Abstract: Medical devices are becoming more software-intensive, connected, and AI-enabled. Their development requires risk-management evidence aligned with ISO 14971 and, for software, IEC 62304. This evidence must be kept consistent across requirements, design decisions, software changes, verification results, complaints, and post-market data. These tasks are costly and depend on scarce safety and domain experts.
  Large language models (LLMs) may reduce parts of this effort because medical-device safety work is highly document-based. However, current LLM-based safety-engineering studies often address isolated methods, rely on generic prompting or public examples, and provide limited support for source links, traceability, uncertainty handling, lifecycle updates, and recorded expert review. This limits their use in regulated medical-device development.
  This paper argues that the central research problem is not safety-text generation, but source-linked safety-knowledge support. We propose an evidence-grounded framework that connects device artifacts, controlled knowledge storage and retrieval, method-specific generation of candidate safety items, critique and uncertainty checks, and recorded expert review. The framework prepares, links, checks, and updates candidate safety artifacts for expert decision-making. It does not decide whether a device is safe and does not provide regulatory approval. We also outline an evaluation strategy using non-public or newly built medical-device case studies and expert reference analyses to assess coverage, correctness, relevance, traceability, duplicate rate, unsupported claims, and review effort.

</details>


### [121] [ADEPT: A Unified Framework for Deep Learning Test Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12144)
*Yidi Kao,Shawn Burnham,Tommi Rose Fahy,Ali Ghanbari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍ADEPT，一个统一集成多类深度学习测试充分性度量的工程化框架，旨在降低现有度量工具难以复现、比较和部署的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习测试充分性指标覆盖神经元激活、潜在特征、决策边界和输入分布等不同视角，但现有实现通常是彼此独立的研究原型，具有不一致的安装依赖、数据预处理、执行流程和配置方式。因此，研究者与工程人员往往需要投入大量时间补全工具链，难以开展公平比较和实际应用。

Method: ADEPT在统一工作流下整合代表性指标，包括基于神经元覆盖的度量、惊奇充分性、输入分布覆盖、边界覆盖，以及源代码级和模型级变异得分。框架采用模板化指标接口，并定义扩展点以支持新增度量；同时提供基于YAML的配置管理、预处理缓存复用和结构化结果报告。

Result: 摘要表明，ADEPT形成了一个可复用的统一测试充分性评估平台，使不同指标能够在相对一致的执行、配置和报告机制下运行。其工程设计可减少重复实现预处理与配置流程的工作，并改善指标复现、横向比较和工作流集成的便利性。摘要未给出具体实验数据、运行开销或与既有工具的定量对比结果。

Conclusion: ADEPT的主要贡献是对分散的深度学习测试充分性技术进行标准化工程封装，而非提出新的充分性理论指标。该框架对需要复现、评估、扩展或部署现有测试充分性度量的研究和开发人员具有实用价值；其实际效果仍需结合完整论文中的支持指标范围、实验验证、可扩展性和性能评估进一步判断。

Abstract: Over the past decade, many test adequacy metrics have been proposed for deep learning that characterize test dataset adequacy from different perspectives, e.g., neuron activation behavior, latent feature coverage, decision-boundary exploration, etc. However, these metrics are typically released as independent research prototypes with substantially different installation and preprocessing requirements, execution workflows, and configuration mechanisms. These complications make them quite difficult to reproduce, compare, and adopt in research work and practical deployment alike. In this paper, we present the engineering details of ADEPT, a framework that integrates representative adequacy techniques, including neuron-coverage-based metrics, surprise adequacy, input distribution coverage, boundary coverage, and source- and model-level mutation score, under a consistent execution workflow. ADEPT provides a template-based metric interface with well-defined extension points for integrating new adequacy metrics. Furthermore, it provides YAML-based configuration management, preprocessing-cache reuse, and structured result reporting, making it easy to use in any research and development workflows. ADEPT is designed for researchers and practitioners who wish to reproduce and apply adequacy metrics without spending days or weeks implementing missing tooling or configuring disparate research prototypes. A demo video is available at https://aub.ie/ADEPT_video.

</details>


### [122] [Towards Automated Domain Model Extraction from Source Code using Heuristics and Open-Source LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12228)
*Alessandra Mancas,Mounir Ammam,Hyacinth Ali,Kevin Delcourt,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向本地轻量级大语言模型的自动化领域模型提取方法，通过分阶段分析源代码中的关键元素，在无法一次处理大型代码库的条件下实现领域概念识别与边界推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能专有大语言模型虽适合代码理解，但在隐私和保密要求较高的工业场景中难以使用；可本地部署的开源小型模型则受限于上下文窗口，无法直接分析完整大型代码库。因此，需要一种兼顾本地部署、数据保密与大规模代码分析能力的领域模型逆向工程方案。

Method: 方法结合结构启发式规则、语义启发式规则与迭代式大语言模型推理。首先对代码元素进行排序和筛选，再逐步将优先级较高的代码子集输入轻量级模型，识别候选领域概念；随后通过多轮推理持续补充、合并或修正概念，并细化概念之间的领域边界。该渐进式流程避免了对完整系统上下文的直接依赖。

Result: 作者在包含十个项目的数据集上进行评估，每个项目均具有人工整理的领域模型及对应实现。结果表明，该方法在领域模型提取任务中取得较高的F1分数，同时可完全运行于本地部署的轻量级大语言模型。摘要未提供具体模型、F1数值、基线方法及误差分析。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，借助代码结构与语义线索，以及面向局部代码上下文的迭代推理，轻量级本地模型能够有效支持领域模型逆向工程。其核心价值在于降低对专有云端模型和全局上下文窗口的依赖，适用于对源码数据控制要求严格的工业软件维护与理解场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown strong capabilities for code understanding, making them promising for reverse engineering domain models from source code. However, state-ofthe- art proprietary LLMs cannot be used in many industrial contexts due to privacy and confidentiality constraints, while compact open-source LLMs that can run locally are limited by their context window and cannot process large code bases directly. In this paper, we propose an automated approach to extract domain models from source code using lightweight, locally deployable LLMs. Our method combines structural and semantic heuristics with iterative LLM-based reasoning to overcome context limitations. By progressively analyzing ranked subsets of code elements, the approach identifies domain concepts and refines domain boundaries without requiring full-system context. Our approach achieves high F1-scores on a dataset of ten projects, each comprising a curated domain model and its corresponding implementation, while remaining fully executable on locally deployable LLMs. This makes it particularly suitable for reverse engineering tasks in privacy-sensitive industrial environments.

</details>


### [123] [The Role Specialization Model (RSM): Coordinating LLM-Based Tools in Agentic Software Development - An Exploratory Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12311)
*Carlos Alberto Fernández-y-Fernández,Jorge R. Aguilar-Cisneros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出并初步验证“角色专门化模型”（RSM），用于协调多个能力不同的大语言模型工具参与端到端软件开发。案例显示，显式角色分工有助于组织开发流程与改善架构质量，但仍依赖上下文管理、提示词强化和人工验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型的自主代理进入软件工程全生命周期，单一工具难以稳定覆盖架构设计、编码、命令行执行和质量验证等异质任务。论文旨在探索如何通过明确的角色分配协调多种工具，并评估这种协作在真实开发流程中的偏差与产物质量。

Method: 研究采用探索性案例研究：以增量开发一个用于交互式气候数据可视化的Python桌面应用为任务，协调Antigravity、Gemini CLI和本地运行的Qwen Code。作者提出RSM，为工具分配不同职责，并围绕三项问题记录工作流执行、计划角色与实际行为的偏差、偏差成因、提示词强化策略，以及依据ISO/IEC 25010开展的定性质量评估。

Result: 研究表明，RSM能够将不同模型工具纳入较清晰的开发分工，支持开发周期组织和架构层面的质量控制。但执行中会出现角色越界、能力重叠或任务偏离等现象，可能与工具能力差异、上下文限制、指令不充分及代理输出不稳定有关。通过更严格的提示词、任务边界定义和人工复核可缓解部分问题。

Conclusion: 该工作主要贡献是提出RSM并以案例展示多代理软件开发协作的可行性。其结论具有探索性：角色协调并不能自动保证高质量交付，可靠实践仍需要显式编排、持续上下文维护、验证机制和人类监督。由于仅基于单一应用案例及定性评估，未来应通过更多项目、量化指标和对照实验验证模型的普适性与实际效益。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into software development workflows has given rise to a paradigm known as Agentic Software Engineering (SE 3.0), in which autonomous agents manage full development life cycles under human supervision. This paper presents an exploratory case study in which three LLM-based tools, Antigravity (an agentic IDE with a Gemini 2.5 backend), Gemini CLI, and Qwen Code (local execution via Ollama), are coordinated according to a role-distribution framework proposed in this work as the Role Specialization Model (RSM). Three research questions guide the study: (RQ1) how can LLM-based tools with distinct capabilities be coordinated through the RSM in a real development workflow; (RQ2) what deviations from the planned role distribution emerge during RSM execution and what factors explain them; and (RQ3) how does the resulting product compare against the ISO/IEC 25010 quality model. The objective was to propose the RSM with the incremental development of a Python desktop application for interactive climate-data visualization. The workflow, observed deviations, prompt-hardening techniques, and a qualitative quality assessment are documented. Results suggest that explicit role coordination can support development cycle organization and architectural quality, but requires deliberate coordination strategies, context management, and human verification of agent-generated outputs.

</details>
